Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the markov_chains space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 436 and the convergence rate is O(861/n^3).

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^861(S, F) is trivial. QED.
